This was a BAND-AID\n202|# for an upstream bug that is now fixed at the root, and on the canonical affine\n203|# DeepSeek-V4-Flash checkpoint it CORRUPTS output.\n204|#\n205|# History: the batched L>1 spec-VERIFY forward used to be numerically inaccurate\n206|# (hand-rolled bf16 split-softmax), so its argmax flipped vs a sequential decode\n207|# at tied logits → spurious </think> / degeneration. The tie-break (deterministic\n208|# lowest-id-within-eps on the bonus token, eps=0.5) papered over that on the OLD\n209|# bundled-MTP checkpoint, whose logits were sharp enough (top-2 gap > 0.5) that\n210|# the eps window only ever caught genuine ~1ulp ties.\n211|#\n212|# Two things changed: (1) upstream mlx-lm 491f6fe/5b00004 routes the L>1 verify\n213|# through the accurate fp32 fused sparse SDPA — verify argmax is now bit-faithful\n214|# to a clean reference forward (confirmed cycle-by-cycle via EXO_DSV4_MTP_REFCHECK:\n215|# verify_argmax == reference_argmax every cycle). The flip the tie-break existed\n216|# to fix no longer happens. (2) The canonical affine checkpoint has FLATTER logits\n217|# (observed top-2 gaps 0.375–0.5), so eps=0.5 now captures the true argmax PLUS\n218|# lower-id distractor tokens and the \"pick lowest id\" rule emits the WRONG token\n219|# every cycle (e.g. true argmax 111467 → emitted 68599), cascading into total\n220|# garbage (\"petabits\", Burmese unicode) at 0% draft acceptance.\n221|#\n222|# Root-cause fix = trust the now-accurate verify argmax; leave the tie-break off.\n223|# (If a genuine ~1ulp tie cascade ever resurfaces, re-enable with an eps on the\n224|# order of a bf16 ulp at logit scale, ~1e-2 — NOT 0.5.)\n225|: \"${EXO_DSV4_MTP_TIEBREAK_FIX:=0}\"\n226|: \"${EXO_DSV4_MTP_TIEBREAK_EPS:=0.5}\"\n227|# Greedy accept-rule alignment (2026-07-10): argmax over logsumexp-normalized\n228|# logprobs (the MTP-off generator's rule) instead of raw verify_logits for the\n229|# temp=0 accept/bonus decisions — removes the near-tie trajectory divergence\n230|# between MTP-on and MTP-off (the mismatch the retired tie-break fix papered\n231|# over).
